- **place_descriptions** (table) — place_id text, lang text, text text, import_source text, import_ref text, import_version text
- **place_footprints** (table) — id integer, place_id text, role text, geom USER-DEFINED, period int8range, certainty text, accuracy_m real, citekey text
- **place_links** (table) — place_id text, scheme text, external_id text, relation text, import_source text, import_ref text, import_version text, item_class text, item_geom USER-DEFINED, item_period int8range
- **place_names** (table) — place_id text, name text, lang text, period int8range, manual boolean, import_source text, import_ref text, import_version text, citation text
- **place_overrides** (table) — place_id text, kind text, period int8range, reason text, source_kind text, source_period int8range, source_start_unknown boolean, created_at timestamp with time zone, updated_at timestamp with time zone, point USER-DEFINED, source_geom USER-DEFINED
- **place_relations** (table) — child_id text, parent_id text, kind text, period int8range
- **place_removals** (table) — id text, label text, reason text, removed_at timestamp with time zone
- **place_types** (table) — place_id text, type_label text, period int8range, citekey text
- **places** (table) — id text, label text, kind text, geom USER-DEFINED, period int8range, source text, properties jsonb, geom_clipped USER-DEFINED, uid uuid, origin uuid, dataset text, updated_at timestamp with time zone, import_source text, import_ref text, import_version text, start_unknown boolean, location_precision text, site_type text, site_type_checked_at timestamp with time zone, shape_area double precision, shape_box USER-DEFINED, shape_dim smallint, shape_valid boolean, label_norm text, web_geojson text
- **plan_pools** (table) — pool text, held boolean, floors text, refusal text, refusal_until timestamp with time zone, plan_type text, updated_at timestamp with time zone
- **plan_quota_readings** (table) — pool text, limit_id text, kind text, model text, used_pct double precision, resets_at timestamp with time zone, rejected boolean, observed_at timestamp with time zone, source text
- **plan_quota_refreshes** (table) — id bigint, pool text, at timestamp with time zone, mode text, trigger text, ok boolean, detail text
- **queue_items** (table) — id integer, list text, text text, topic text, status text, created_at timestamp with time zone, done_at timestamp with time zone, reason text, uid uuid, origin uuid, updated_at timestamp with time zone, goal_slug text, created_job text, closed_job text
- **read_marks** (table) — id uuid, user_id text, target_kind text, target_id text, target_uid uuid, target_label text, read_at timestamp with time zone, opened_at timestamp with time zone, origin uuid, created_at timestamp with time zone, updated_at timestamp with time zone
- **retraction_watch** (table) — doi text, reason text, date date, nature text
- **review_ledgers** (table) — slug text, ledger jsonb, updated_at timestamp with time zone
- **seed_versions** (table) — name text, version integer, applied_at timestamp with time zone
- **site_undated_build** (table) — id boolean, version bigint, per_side integer, max_zoom integer, built_at timestamp with time zone
- **site_undated_cells** (table) — z smallint, gx bigint, gy bigint, site_type text, n integer, sx double precision, sy double precision, w double precision, s double precision, e double precision, nn double precision, one text
- **source_aliases** (table) — alias text, citekey text, created_at timestamp with time zone
- **source_candidates** (table) — id bigint, goal text, doi text, title text, year integer, authors ARRAY, venue text, abstract text, is_oa boolean, arxiv_id text, found_by ARRAY, state text, first_seen timestamp with time zone, last_seen timestamp with time zone
- **source_chunks** (table) — citekey text, ord integer, heading text, body text, tsv tsvector
- **source_identifiers** (table) — source_uid uuid, scheme text, value text
- **sources** (table) — citekey text, doi text, title text, authors ARRAY, year integer, venue text, status text, topics ARRAY, regions ARRAY, tags ARRAY, period int8range, cited_by_count integer, influential_citations integer, summary text, ocr_markdown text, source text, calibre_id text, sha256 text, added_date date, updated_at timestamp with time zone, notes text, tsv tsvector, uid uuid, origin uuid, license text
- **tombstones** (table) — uid uuid, kind text, origin uuid, deleted_at timestamp with time zone
- **ui_seen** (table) — board text, seen_at timestamp with time zone
- **uid_aliases** (table) — alias_uid uuid, uid uuid, kind text, merge_id uuid, created_at timestamp with time zone
- **unresolved_edges** (table) — src text, dst text, kind text
- **upstream_quota** (table) — upstream text, day date, lookup_tokens integer, search_tokens integer, blocked_until timestamp with time zone, blocked_why text, rate_limit integer, rate_remaining integer, observed_at timestamp with time zone, updated_at timestamp with time zone
